Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the p-adic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 611 and the convergence rate is O(667/n^1).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on p-adic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 611. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 667. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the p-adic structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 800. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
